市場焦點
聯準會9月
升息一碼
• 聯邦基準利率升至 3.75%~4.00%，經濟預測部分上調2026-2028年GDP成長率、失業率皆下
調，通膨降溫進程仍相對緩慢 ，點陣圖顯示今年還有升息一碼的機會 ，這次升息對股票而言 ，
聯準會沒有準備以大幅犧牲經濟與就業換取通膨下降 ，降低了硬著陸風險；對債券而言 ，短
端仍須消化更高的政策利率，但長端反而可能受惠於抗通膨可信度改善。
AI模型
開發放緩
• Anthropic、OpenAI及xAI相繼支持放緩最先進AI模型開發速度。不過，前沿模型開發速度放
緩，並不代表AI基礎建設需求同步轉弱。晶片、算力、資料中心及能源等AI基礎建設仍處於供
不應求格局 ，短期若因市場情緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔 ，較可能反映估值與持倉修正 ，

而非AI需求基本面反轉 。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化 ，且全球總體經濟及地緣政治
風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。
台灣央行
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融
通利率分別維持2%、2.375%和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制 ，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最
高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通膨率 ，GDP上修至11.48%、CPI
及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16。(4)央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表
現，不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。

資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17
貨幣決議與9月、7月聲明稿差異，不再強調能源衝擊 全員支持升息
• 聯準會9月升息一碼，聯邦基準利率在3.75%~4.00%。
聲明稿重點差異
紅色字體為9月的新增部分，括號內藍色字體為刪除的7月聲明措辭
FOMC以12票贊成升息一碼 批准發布以下聲明 ：委員會決定 ，將聯邦基金利率目標 區間上調至 3.75%至4%維持在
3.50%至3.75%，以支持聯準會的雙重使命。委員會延續其維持銀行體系充足準備金的政策。
儘管中東衝突在一定程度上帶來了高水準的不確定性 ，美國經濟活動持續以穩健步調擴張 。儘管地緣政治情勢 等因素
使經濟前景仍籠 罩高度不確定性，但國內支出保持韌性，生產力成長強勁，企業資本投資也相當強勁穩健。新增就業

人數大致跟上勞動力成長速度，失業率也變化不大，顯示就業市場目前仍大致維持穩定。
相較於委員會2%的目標，通貨膨脹率依然偏高 ，這在一定程度上反映了部分特定部門 (包括能源)因供給衝擊所導致的
價格上漲。今日的政策行動，將有助於讓通膨更及時地回到2%目標，委員會將實現價格穩定。

聯準會會後記者會問答重點整理
1
通膨「過高且持續太久」 當
前重心偏向物價穩定
通膨數據未改善，通膨過高且持續太久，聯準會當前的政策重心偏向雙重使命中的物價穩定 。透過政策
行動表明，聯準會對抑制通膨是認真的。
2
經濟、就業與地緣政治促成
一致升息
近幾週公布的數據證實美國經濟正在走強 ，基礎經濟成長率高於先前判斷；其次 ，勞動市場依然穩健；
第三，通膨持續高於目標，地緣政治局勢也改變經濟展望 。華許表示，這三項因素共同促成當天「堅定
且一致」的決定。
3
點狀圖暗示今年再升 1碼 但
不是政策承諾
最新經濟預測顯示，多數官員認為今年仍有進一步升息空間。12 名官員預期年底前再升息 1 碼，另有 4
人預期還會升息 2 碼，僅 2 人主張利率維持在目前水準。不過，點狀圖反映的是官員對適當利率路徑的

個別預測，並非正式的承諾。
4
拒絕提供前瞻指引 不預判下
次會議結果
面對下一次升息可能落在 10 月或 12 月的追問，華許表示「我不給出前瞻指引。」
他強調，先前公開表達的是對貨幣政策紀律的承諾 ，而不是對特定利率決策的承諾 。聯準會不會預判未
來任何會議結果，後續行動仍將依據最新經濟數據及展望作出決定。
5
升息非市場逼迫 迴避川普施
壓問題
面對此次升息是否由債市波動及市場壓力所推動的提問，華許予以否認。
他表示是根據就業走勢、經濟強度及整體情勢作出決定 ，市場價格可作為觀察依據，但不能決定聯準會
的政策。
資料來源：Fed，華爾街見聞，2026.9.17

市場反應與投資建議
9/16
市場反應
• 市場將華許記者會解讀為偏鷹，那斯達克指數、標普
500指數、道瓊工業指數皆下跌。
• 10 年 期 美 債 殖 利 率 在 利 率 決 議 公 布 後 一 度 降 至
4.9323%，隨後回升至接近 4.99%，在關鍵的5%關
口附近波動。
• 2年期殖利率則由盤中低點4.5975%升至4.7124%，
反映投資人重新提高對後續升息的預期。
• 黃金跌破4,260美元。
• 利率市場顯示 ，交易員加大 聯準會在2026 年底前進
一步升息的押注，12月升息機率升至約50%。
投資建議
• 股市：AI長期趨勢不變，但需留意利率對評價面的影
響，逢拉回就分批買進，相對看好美、台、韓相關供
應鏈
• 債市：預期美債利率 維持高位震盪格局 ，目前債市

配置仍以鎖利為主，且聚焦中短天期。
資料來源：芝商所，2026.9.17

台灣利率連10凍 明年經濟展望佳
• 利率決議重點： (1)台灣央行宣布基準利率維持不變 ，重貼現率、擔保放款融通利率和短期融通利率分別維持 2%、2.375%
和4.25%。(2)放鬆房市信用管制，調整自然人第2戶購屋貸款最高成數上限，由6成調升為7成。(3)上修今年經濟成長率及通
膨率，GDP上修至11.48%(六月預估9.45 %)、CPI及核心CPI分別上修至2.03%及2.16%(六月預估1.91%及1.90%)。
• 觀點：全球AI資本支出維持上調的趨勢，台股供應鏈訂單直接受惠，央行預估明年AI基礎建設需求將持續貢獻台灣出口表現，
不過由於基期已墊高，預測明年台灣經濟成長率為5.82%。
• 台股短線展望：上週隨著聯準會利率決議公布後，台股量能回升，顯示短線消息面或已利空出盡。權值股台積電領軍台灣加

權指數站上月線，技術指標偏正向。投資佈局方面，由於地緣政治、通膨及貿易關稅風險仍存在不確定性，建議AI概念持股
核心布局行業龍頭企業，留意中小型成長股較易受到外部因素而股價大幅波動。
資料來源：中央銀行，2026.9.17。f代表央行預測數。

美國零售強勁回升擴大內需支撐，惟價格壓力與升息提高市場波動
• 零售全面回升，消費動能展現廣度：美國8月零售銷售月增1.2%，創3月以來最大增幅，優於市場預期，並扭轉7月修正
後月減0.5%；非實體店面銷售月增 2.6%、電子及家電增 1.6%，餐飲服務亦增 1.2%，且扣除汽車與汽油後仍增 1.2%，
控制組更增1.4%，顯示消費回升並非僅由汽車與燃料支出帶動，第三季內需仍具支撐。
• 名目消費偏強，通膨與升息增添需求壓力： 不過8月零售銷售年增6.0%屬名目增幅，部分反映物價上升因素；同期 CPI
年增3.4%，汽油價格月增3.9%，能源成本上升可能壓縮家庭實質購買力 ，加上Fed於9月決議升息1碼，後續利率政策
仍將視通膨與經濟數據調整，使市場焦點由消費回升轉向評估高利率與高物價對後續需求動能的影響。

• 後市觀察：短期零售回升仍有助支撐美國景氣，但若能源成本持續走高，加上升息後借貸成本上升，消費動能能否維持
將成為重要觀察點；投資配置可偏向美國投資級短中天期債券 ，兼顧收益與利率風險，股票則可聚焦獲利穩定、具定價
能力的大型優質企業，降低需求放緩與資金成本升高的影響。
近三月美國零售銷售月率變化(%)

科技企業自由現金流可望由目前約2,450億增至2028年的6,330億美元，支撐亞洲科技供應鏈中長期發展。短期若因市場情

緒或資金槓桿調整而出現回檔，較可能反映估值與持倉修正，而非AI需求基本面反轉。後續若前期累積的槓桿壓力逐步消化，
且全球總體經濟及地緣政治風險未進一步惡化，AI供應鏈仍具中長期配置價值。

油價在美國中期選舉前，或將維持在高檔
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.14
伊朗戰爭期間卡達油氣收入暴跌
卡達政府的石油和天然氣收入 美伊衝突
• 美伊衝突短期內仍難見明確解方，彭博預估雙方後續可能採取的行動，主要可分為以下四種情境：
(1)伊朗承受經濟封鎖：伊朗承受美國經濟制裁與施壓，避免進一步重大軍事升級。在美方護航行動支持下，商船仍可維持通行
荷姆茲海峽，國際原油供應未出現明顯中斷，油價預估在每桶80美元區間波動。
(2)伊朗將攻擊範圍限於海灣地區油輪與美軍：伊朗主要鎖定海灣地區能源運輸船隻及美軍目標，美方則採取有限度軍事回應，

衝突維持在一定範圍內，油價預估在每桶90美元區間波動。
(3)伊朗擴大攻擊範圍：攻擊目標不再局限於海灣地區船隻及美軍基地，海灣能源基礎設施以及紅海等重要航運咽喉，均可能成
為目標。局勢升級期間，油價可能維持在每桶100美元以上，待衝突緩和後回落至70至80美元區間，此情境發生的可能性最高。
(4)美伊雙方全面擴大開戰：伊朗進一步擴大攻擊目標，導致美國軍事資產及區域能源基礎設施遭受重大破壞，甚至不排除以色
列重新加入戰局，油價可能升至每桶120美元。

全球債市拋售勢頭不墜，AI融資競逐資金推升美債殖利率
• 長端殖利率走高，財政與通膨壓力仍是主因 ：近期美國長
天期公債殖利率持續走高，其中10年期美債殖利率更突破
5%，創2007年以來高點；除能源價格上升推升通膨疑慮
外，美國財政赤字、政府債務及持續性的發債需求 ，也提
高市場對長天期公債供給與期限溢酬的要求 ，反映長端利
率壓力仍未完全紓解。
• AI企業擴大融資，增加公司債供給與資金競逐： AI資料中
心、晶片與電力基礎建設的資本支出快速增加 ，帶動大型
科技企業提高債券融資規模；截至 6月，華爾街市場策略
研究機構Yardeni Research在追蹤外國私人投資人過去一
年買進美國公司債約3,900億美元，高於美國公債約3,290
億美元，顯示資金配置確有轉向公司債的現象 ，不過市場

研究亦指出，AI企業發債對美債殖利率的直接推升效果相
對有限，應可視為推動長天期利率上行的眾多因素之一。
• 後市觀察：若財政融資與AI基礎建設支出持續維持高檔 ，
長天期公債仍面臨較大的供給與資金配置壓力；但 AI投資
仍具中長期成長動能 ，配置上可關注資料中心 、半導體及
電力基礎建設供應鏈 ，固定收益則以短中天期美債及高品
質投資級公司債為主，降低長天期利率波動的影響。

新興市場本幣債券擁高票息提供防禦力
• 高票息提供防禦力：對債券投資人而言，票息就像安全墊。當市場利率上升、債券價格下跌時，持續收到的票息可以部分甚
至完全抵銷價格損失。新興市場本幣政府債券今年的表現，比市場原先預期更具韌性。目前新興市場本幣債券的持有收益約
為4.54%，已經足以對利率風險形成相當程度的保護 ，即使今年存續期間對多數市場造成負面影響 ，票息收益仍讓整體報酬
維持正數。這也是為什麼新興市場本幣債券在美伊衝突爆發後一度重挫超過 6%，但之後仍能逐步反彈 。哥倫比亞、巴西、
匈牙利及墨西哥的債券表現尤其突出。
• 市場已反映相當程度的升息風險：問題在於未來幾個月投資環境可能比上半年更加困難，能源價格上漲推升通膨，也迫使新
興市場央行維持較為鷹派的貨幣政策 。市場目前甚至預期 ，19個主要新興市場中 ，有18個市場的債券殖利率可能在年底前

上升。殖利率上升意味著債券價格承壓，因此投資人不能只看表面的高票息，必須同時評估未來利率變化可能造成資本損失。
新興市場本幣債券各因子對報酬率貢獻
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15
新興市場本幣債券票息的貢獻度

新興市場本幣債券目前最大風險在於利率與匯率
• 存續期間仍是今年最主要的拖累因素：一般而言，債券的存續期間越高，當殖利率上升時，債券價格下跌的幅度就可能越大。
今年19個符合基準指數條件的新興市場中 ，有14個市場的總報酬受到存續期間拖累 ，其中韓國、馬來西亞、印尼、泰國及
菲律賓受到的負面影響最為明顯。更值得注意的是，新興市場債券的利率表現與美國公債高度相關，因此，投資新興市場債
券，不能只觀察當地央行，也必須密切留意美國聯準會利率政策及美國公債殖利率走勢。
• 匯率可能成為下一個重要變數：新興市場本幣債券除了債券價格本身，還存在另一項重要風險：匯率。今年即期匯率變動反
而對新興市場本幣債券帶來正面貢獻 ，約增加57個基點。但如果能源價格持續上漲並形成滯脹環境 ，順周期貨幣可能面臨

更大的貶值壓力 。目前市場預期年底前新興市場貨幣整體仍偏弱 ，19種新興市場貨幣中 ，有11種預計兌美元貶值 。其中土
耳其、哥倫比亞及巴西貨幣的潛在跌幅相對較大。這代表即使債券本身提供不錯的票息，如果當地貨幣大幅貶值，換算成美
元或其他投資人本國貨幣後，仍可能侵蝕甚至吞噬債券收益。
• 小結與投資建議：目前新興市場本幣債券比較像是高票息換取較高波動的投資，而不是單純追求資本利得的交易。在殖利率
已處於相對高檔、但利率與匯率風險仍未完全解除的情況下，採取分散市場、控制部位、逢回布局的策略會比追高更為合適。

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.15

• 多重資產配置可有效風險管理：美伊衝突持續及聯準會利率政策，使今年金融市場震盪加劇，加上第四季美國期中選舉到來，
增加市場更多不確定性，不過從歷史經驗來看，地緣政治事件對股市的影響多屬短期現象，市場在初期情緒性下跌後，通常會
逐步回歸基本面，相較於預測事件發展，投資人更應透過多元資產配置分散風險，以提升投資組合的穩定性與長期抗震能力。
• 透過多重資產廣納收益來源：在利率維持相對高檔的環境下，投資人更應重視收益來源的多元化，從下圖可見，相較於過去幾
年，掩護性買權、非投資等級債，及投資等級債等資產的收益率均顯著提升，提供投資組合更具吸引力的現金流來源，其中掩
護性買權收益率已超過10%，非投資等級債，及投資等級債亦分別達7%及5%以上水準，透過結合不同收益型資產，不僅有助

提升整體收益潛力，更能降低對單一市場或利率變動的依賴，在市場波動加劇時提高投資組合的穩定度與韌性，兼顧收益與長
期成長目標。

7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

TSMC-Musk: customer relationship intact; 
tech/control sharing with Terafab unlikely

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,500 TWD

Customer neutrality central to TSMC’s foundry model 
Elon Musk on Saturday October 3rd responded “Just discussions, but something may come 
of it” to an article discussing the potential manufacturing partnership between TSMC 
and Terafab. From chipset design and foundry service perspective, we expect a deeper 
collaboration as Tesla and SpaceX grow at TSMC (2-3% of its sales) directly with their 
auto/robotics and cloud AI ASICs and indirectly through merchant HPC chipset buys (e.g. 
GPUs, networking switches) based on our estimate. However, the likelihood of TSMC 
licensing technology or operating a fab with Terafab holding a meaningful share should 
be low. Specifically, we would note TSMC’s JV fab record is limited including WaferTech, 
SSMC, VIS, JASM, ESMC and AVSM mostly on mature nodes. The company did not co-
own a fab with Apple who contributed ~21% of its revenue from ’15-25 mostly on the 
advanced processes as it keeps full business control and preserves customer neutrality. 
Terafab capex insufficient for 1 terawatt capacity target 
Based on Terafab’s applications under Texas’s JETI program, it outlined ~US$119bn of 
planned investment across 4 phases from ’26-36, with ~60% of it on tool procurement. 
The spending in ’26-28 would be highly skewed toward facility construction before it 
starts to pull in US$6-8bn equipment per annum from ‘29-36. Based on our estimate of 
US$600mn+ for every 1k WPM expansion on the advanced nodes in the US, Terafab 
could only grow its capacity installed base by ~10k WPM per annum at maximum even if 
it can successfully deliver the technology roadmap through its own R&D effort and Intel 
partnership. This would prevent Terafab’s operation from the economies of scale as the 
node migration continues, keeping TSMC as the major foundry partner for Tesla/SpaceX. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We keep our Buy rating on TSMC with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E (vs. current 16x).

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

### Page 1

King Slide

Maintain Buy: Sep sales -31% MoM from 
high bases; Aug earnings remain solid

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 14,888 TWD | Price: 13,100 TWD

Sep sales -31% MoM from high bases; 3Q26 sales in-line 
King Slide’s Sep sales of NT$4.46bn declined 31% MoM (+196% YoY), after the strong 
sales in the past few months (up 17-46% MoM in Apr-Jul and up 1% MoM in Aug). 3Q26 
sales of NT$17.34bn (+60% QoQ, +297% YoY) were 0.5% above BofAe of NT$17.26bn 
and 0.9% below consensus of NT$17.49bn.  
We are not surprised with the MoM sales drop in Sep as (1) the company’s MoM sales 
trend has been volatile (Exhibit 1 and Exhibit 2), (2) sales bases in Apr-Aug were at 
record levels, (3) contribution from Nvidia Rubin GPU projects should be more 
meaningful in 4Q26 after hardware components ramp-up, and (4) partial general server 
orders/demand likely were pushed out due to key component shortage (e.g. memory, 
CPU). 
Solid Aug EPS NT$40.07; pre-tax/net margin 74.6%/59.1% 
In addition to Sep sales, on the same day (6 Oct), King Slide also announced its Aug 
unaudited key financial data as required by local regulations (Exhibit 3). Aug sales were 
NT$6,466mn (+344% YoY), while pre-tax profit was NT$4,826mn (+216% YoY) and net 
income was NT$3,819mn (+212% YoY). EPS came in at NT$40.07 (+212% YoY). Pre-tax 
margin and net margin were 74.6% and 59.1%, respectively.  
Compared to July unaudited figures announced on 14 Aug (see our 17 Aug report), 
including sales of NT$6,407mn, pre-tax margin of 93.4%, net margin of 74.7%, and EPS 
of NT$50.24, we believe the margin and EPS differences mainly reflected FX impact 
under similar sales scale. 
Combining Jul and Aug unaudited results, sales totaled NT$12.87bn and EPS reached 
NT$90.31. Pre-tax margin and net margin were 84.0% and 66.9%, respectively. Jul + Aug 
sales achieved 75%/74% of BofAe/consensus 3Q26 sales. Pre-tax profit, net income, 
and EPS all achieved 79% of BofAe (79.4% pre-tax margin, 62.9% net margin, and 
NT$113.87 EPS) and 77-78% of consensus (80.3% pre-tax margin, 63.1% net margin, 
and NT$117.62 EPS). We also noted that Jul + Aug EPS of NT$90.31 already surpassed 
EPS of NT$74.38 in 2Q26 with similar pre-tax/net margin (84.0%/66.9% in Jul + Aug, vs. 
84.2%/65.4% in 2Q26). 
Maintain Buy; expect a neutral-to-positive market reaction 
We expect a neutral-to-positive market reaction in the near term in view of significant 
MoM sales decline in Sep but also still solid Aug unaudited data. We maintain Buy on 
King Slide as the server rail market leader with broad and diversified client/project base. 
We expect the company to post 3Q26 results after market close on 12 Nov (Thu).

Price objective basis & risk 
King Slide (KGSLF) 
Our PO of NT$14,888 is based on 32x 2027E EPS. We use 32x P/E multiple (2.5SD 
above P/E average since listing) in view of strong AI server market demand, leading 
position in server rail, solid/high margin profiles, and double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 
2026-28E. Its early step into server market since 2001 with proven track record, much 
larger scale than peer, and 3,600+ patents as strong moats for high/above-peer margins. 
Its above-30x P/E is a similar case to other component sub-sector leaders in Taiwan.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) even stronger AI server demand, (2) margin upside thanks 
to larger scale and/or better efficiency, (3) better kitchen slides market demand, (4) 
faster capacity expansion, (5) favorable FX movement (mainly USD/NTD) for GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/components 
shortage, (2) GM pressure due to higher raw material cost (e.g. steel, aluminum), (3) 
softer kitchen slides market demand under macro uncertainty/weakness, (4) delayed 
product certification, (5) slower capacity expansion, (6) unfavorable FX movement 
(mainly USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

Largan reported 3Q26 net income of NT$5.182bn, down 27% YoY.
The company reported a 11% YoY revenue decline, to NT$15.651bn; gross margin
declined YoY and QoQ to 42.2% in 3Q26, lower than expected due to lower new-
project yields and structural green electricity cost hikes.
Management expects shipments to be flat MoM in October and decline

sequentially in November as order momentum is weaker-than-expected.

verification; expanding from FA into FAU, including coupling and potential FAU-to-
carrier assembly, with a dedicated new facility expected to support mass production
from around mid-2027. We maintain our long-term positive view on Largan's FAU/
CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional business
and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual income
model, consistent with our Greater China Hardware Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a 7% net profit CAGR over 2026-36E and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately

value the FAU business, assuming a 40% market share in 2028.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
n Stronger-than-expected CPO volume growth
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

n Weaker-than-expected CPO volume growth

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3Q26/4Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 主要成長動能來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及
AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，高階產品規格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升，其中
1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以上。BT 載板則持續
受惠記憶體需求強勁。樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產能均已接近全面
滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率 60%–70%以上，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。本中心
預估 3Q/4Q26 營收 163.8/188.5 億元，季增 20.6%/15.1% ，毛利率
30%/32.3%，稅後獲利 35/46.3 億元，EPS 5.43/7.16 元。 
玻纖布、CCL 持續漲價，預期 4Q26–2027 年載板價格上漲 
經本中心供應鏈訪查了解，高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，使部分 BT 料
件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上；ABF 隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上
Build-up 結構發展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；
玻纖布方面， Nittobo 新增玻纖布供給已大致完成客戶分配，短期缺口難以
明顯緩解。隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，
持續消耗載板廠商有效產能。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價由規格升級、單顆材
料用量增加與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至
2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
2027 年營運動能明顯增強，給予買進評等，目標價 1,700 元 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過 468 億元資本支出計畫，主要鎖定大尺寸、高層數、
高精密度 IC 載板。在高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動
下，預期 ABF/BT 毛利率可望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。
本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1,095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，
毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。本中心給予南電買進評等，以
2027 年 EPS，30 倍本益比推得目標價 1,700 元。

元大觀點 
⧫  
台灣載板產能接近全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率提升至 60%–70%且
4Q26 有望滿載，預估 3Q/4Q26 營收季增 20.6%/15.1%。 
⧫  
規格升級與高階 產品及上游原料 產能不足共同驅動載板漲價，預期
ABF/BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。 
⧫  
高階產品比重提升、稼 動率維持高檔及報價上調，給予 南電 買進評
等，目標價 1,700 元，以 2027 年 EPS 56.78 元及 30 倍本益比推估。

營運分析 
3Q26 ABF、BT 稼動率持續向上，預期帶動營運成長 
南電 3Q26 營收 163.8 億元，季增 20.6%，年增 49.3%，主要成長動能仍來自於 ABF/BT，ABF 載
板方面，受惠 1.6T 高階交換器晶片及 AI ASIC 專案陸續放量，除出貨量持續增加外，高階產品規
格升級亦帶動平均銷售單價提升。其中，1.6T 交換器載板 ASP 相較前一代 800G 產品高出一倍以
上，隨高階產品占比提升，為營收與獲利成長的推力。BT 載板則持續受惠 DRAM 與 NAND Flash
等記憶體需求強勁，加上上游原材料成本上漲逐步反映於產品報價，使 BT 載板呈現量價齊揚。毛
利率預估為 30%，營業利益 43.7 億元，季增 50.6%，年增 604.1%，稅後獲利 35 億元，季增 55%，
年增 383.6%，EPS 5.43 元。

台灣廠全面滿載，昆山 ABF 稼動率快速回升 
產能利用率方面，目前台灣主要載板廠區維持高檔運作，其中樹林廠及錦興廠的 ABF 與 BT 載板產
能均已接近全面滿載，顯示高階載板及記憶體相關需求仍相當強勁。同時，昆山 ABF 廠則為後續
營運改善的重要來源。隨美系網通大廠新專案自 7M26 正式進入量產，昆山 ABF 稼動率已由 2Q26
約 50%快速提升至 60%–70%，進入 4Q26 有望趨於滿載。伴隨稼動率提高及良率改善，昆山廠毛
利率亦顯著提升，預期後續貢獻幅度將提升。PCB 業務，稼動率正由 70%–75%向上，主因 AI 伺
服器板與記憶體模組板需求驅動。綜合上述，預期 4Q26 營收 188.5 億元，季增 15.1%，年增
68.8%，毛利率 32.3%，營業利益 55.1 億元，季增 26.1%，年增 472.1%，稅後獲利 46.3 億元，
季增 32%，EPS 7.16 元

玻纖布、 CCL 等價格仍持續漲價，預期 4Q26-2027 年載板價格上漲 
隨 AI GPU 、ASIC、高速交換器及 HBM 等應用快速成長，IC 載板規格持續朝大尺寸、高層數、細
線路、低翹曲及高耐熱發展，帶動材料由一般電子級產品轉向特種玻纖布、高階 BT 樹脂、ABF 增
層膜等高規材料。經本中心近期供應鏈訪查及日本材料展資訊整理，三菱瓦斯化學（ MGC）指出，
過去 BT 載板主要應用於 DRAM、NAND Flash 及消費性 IC，但隨 HBM、AI ASIC 、網通晶片與
CPO 導入，材料須進一步滿足低 CTE、高耐熱及高速電性需求，高階 BT 與傳統產品的規格差距
持續擴大，目前高階 BT 材料已呈明顯供不應求，部分料件 Lead time 甚至拉長至 70 週以上。玻纖
布方面，Nittobo 目前產能維持 100%滿載，雖規劃 2027–2028 年新增產能，但新增供給已大致完
成客戶分配，短期缺口難以明顯緩解。ABF 方面，隨新世代 AI 載板朝 20 層以上 Build-up 結構發
展，單顆 ABF 膜使用量同步提高，Ajinomoto 供應亦趨緊；同時 Resonac 相關載板材料報價已調
升約 20%–30%。綜合來看，本輪載板漲價並非單純景氣循環，而是規格升級、單顆材料用量增加
與高階有效產能不足共同驅動，預期 ABF／BT 載板於 4Q26 至 2027 年仍具持續漲價空間。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第4 頁，共 19頁  
圖 3：載板面積及層數增加亦在 Ajinomoto 未來載板發展藍圖

資料來源：Ajinomoto，元大投顧整理

南電此前已宣布 468 億元資本支出，為創立以來單筆最大投資 
南電於 8M26 董事會通過新一輪資本支出計畫，總投資金額達 468 億元，為公司成立以來最大單筆
投資案，規模亦超過 2018–2022 年上一輪擴產期間累計資本支出。其中約 110 億元投入廠房土建，
358 億元用於設備採購，新廠定位為具備 Class 100 無塵室等級的高階智慧工廠，主要鎖定大尺寸、
高層數、高精密度 IC 載板，以及 CoWoS、CoPoS 等先進封裝衍生需求。顯示此次投資並非既有
產線單純擴充，而是針對下一世代高階載板重新建置生產平台。整體而言 ，廠區調配方面，錦興廠
P2 升級已於 1H26 完成，主要提升自動化程度與無塵室規格，以因應大型、高階載板生產；樹林
廠則因應 2027 年美系客戶 Server CPU 需求成長，陸續將部分既有成熟產品轉往昆山生產，以釋
放台灣高階產能供新產品使用。

展望 2026-2027 年，ABF/BT 料源及載板的產能供應缺口將推升載板廠商營運 
AI 需求正在從「訓練」往「推論」延伸，這會讓晶片種類變多、數量變大，進而同時推升 IC 載板
的量與規格。此前從 Deep Learning 、Perceptual AI 一路到 Generative AI、Agentic AI，未來再往
Physical AI 發展。訓練（Learning）主要依賴大型模型運算，因此核心晶片以高效能 GPU 為主；
但到了推論（Inference）階段，更重視即時反應速度、功耗與成本，因此除了 GPU 之外，CPU 與
ASIC 的使用比例會明顯提高。展望 2026/2027 年，預計南電載板稼動率持續改善，其中 ABF 與
BT 均已進入高檔運作。ABF 方面，台灣樹林及錦興廠稼動率已接近滿載；昆山廠則為 2H26 稼動
率回升的主要來源，受惠美系網通客戶及中國客戶量產，ABF 稼動率由 2Q26 約 50%快速提升至
3Q26 的 60%–70%，預期 4Q26 在供給持續吃緊下，可進一步提升，2027 年初亦將因應美系客戶
Server CPU 需求快速成長。BT 載板供需則更加緊張，除現有 DRAM、SSD 相關需求持續強勁，
另一方面，部分同業逐步將 BT 產能轉向 ABF，韓系供應商亦優先支應集團內需求，市場有效供給
持續收縮，景碩亦表示稼動率也將逐步向上，預期 2027 年 BT 仍將維持高稼動率。 
綜合上述，本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率
26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年
增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第5 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
AI 需求增溫造成 T-glass 玻纖布供應吃緊，助益近期 BT 載板報價調整 
低熱膨脹係數（Low CTE ）玻璃纖維布，簡稱 T-glass，為高階 AI ABF 載板及高階 BT 載板重要原
料，由 Nittobo(日東紡)獨家供應，並由日系廠商 Resonac(ABF 載板 CCL 主要供應商)、三菱瓦斯
(BT 載板 CCL 主要供應商)採用。由於高階 AI 晶片需求增溫帶動高階 AI ABF 載板出貨成長，造成
T-glass 供應吃緊並連帶使高階 BT 載板 CCL(終端用途包含記憶體控制器、手機 Ap、Sip 等)交貨
期拉長，T-glass 玻纖布供應正常時高階 BT 載板 CCL 交貨期約 8-10 週，後因供應吃緊造成交貨期
拉長至 16-20 週，目前因玻纖布供應更為緊張已無告知交貨期。 
經供應鏈調查，台系載板廠已於 2025 年 7 月開始針對部分採用 T-glass 之 BT 載板產品調漲價格，
此外 BT 載板亦有載板廠進行價格調漲，以反應高階玻纖布供應日漸緊缺，以及年初以來銅價、金
價等原物料成本上漲壓力。由於 BT 載板產業長期以來供過於求，且當前高階 AI 晶片普遍採用
ABF 載板，多數載板廠此前 BT 業務毛利率較為偏低，淡季時季毛利率多有承壓。本中心認為漲價
趨勢可延續，將有助載板廠獲利改善。

圖 4：高階 BT 載板供應鏈示意圖

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第6 頁，共 19頁

圖 6：金價近一年走勢

資料來源：Cmoney、元大投顧整理

中長期高階 ABF 載板價格或能進一步調升 
受惠 AI 發展浪潮帶動 AI GPU 、AI ASIC 等 AI 晶片需求成長，AI ABF 載板需求正向。本中心推測
短期高階 AI ABF 載板因獲利水準足夠暫無調漲壓力，但中長期隨需求持續上升、載板面積增大、
T-glass 供應持續吃緊，AI ABF 載板產品台系廠商後續仍有進一步議價空間。目前欣興、南電、景
碩皆有耕耘高階 ABF 載板，例如欣興在美系 GPU Blackwell 市佔率預估為 30-40%，且為全球主要
AI ASIC 載板供應商；南電今年 800G 載板比重預期約 7%，包含美系、非美系等終端需求；景碩
則有望成為美系 AI GPU 載板第三供。此外部分載板廠因原物料上漲等成本壓力在 2Q25、3Q25 小
幅調整中低階 ABF 載板報價，本中心判斷若 T-glass 供應持續吃緊並排擠 E-glass 產能，則後續中
低階 ABF 載板價格有望進一步提升。

圖 7：高階 ABF 載板供應鏈示意圖

資料來源：元大投顧整理

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第7 頁，共 19頁  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 91%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic) 、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國四大 CSP 資本支出將年增 91%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA GB300 的天下，AWS、Google、
Meta ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將
採購 TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，本中心預估 2026 年通用型伺服器出貨量將年增
23%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP 等業
者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中將消
耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 8

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第8 頁，共 19頁  
ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，NVIDIA
加 AMD GPU 預估年增 17%及 34%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用
GPU 算力需求仍維持成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者， 2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下， CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本， ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

圖 9：GPU/ASIC 出貨量預估

資料來源：元大投顧

AI 晶片面積持續擴大，將使 ABF 載板用量大增 
隨著大型語言模型與生成式 AI 的算力需求快速攀升，AI 晶片的設計持續朝向更高運算密度與更大
面積發展。無論是 NVIDIA、AMD 或各家大廠的新世代加速器，都呈現晶粒尺寸擴大、功耗上升、
I/O 腳位數倍增的趨勢。在此背景下，ABF 載板的需求不僅以「量」擴張，更以「規格」升級為主
軸。為支撐大型 GPU/AI ASIC 的高頻寬連接與多晶片架構，ABF 層數、線寬線距、孔徑控制及翹
曲管理均需同步提升，帶動高端載板技術門檻不斷拉高。由於先進 AI 晶片的封裝面積更大、電源
與訊號整合更複雜，單顆晶片所需 ABF 載板面積與材料用量明顯增加。整體而言，AI 晶片規模擴
大正驅動 ABF 產業進入新一輪升級周期，高階需求將成為未來成長的主要動能。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第10 頁，共 19頁  
獲利調整與股票評價  
給予南電買進評等，目標價 1700 元 
本中心維持 2026–2027 年載板廠商將持續受惠於高稼動率與漲價趨勢的看法不變。主因新世代高
階載板規格持續大型化，傳統 PC／NB 載板尺寸多約 27×27mm，而 Server CPU 、AI ASIC 及高
速交 換器 載板 已逐 步 放大 至 70–80mm 、100mm 以 上， 部分 大型 產品 甚 至 朝 140×140mm 、
150×150mm 發展，未來更大型先進封裝架構更可能逼近 200mm 等級。由於載板生產係以固定尺
寸 Panel 進行製造，隨單顆載板面積放大，每張 Panel 可排入的產品數量將快速下降，同時邊角無
法有效利用所造成的 Area Loss 亦明顯增加，此將持續消耗載板廠商有效產能，有助產業維持高稼
動率，並支撐 2026–2027 年載板報價向上。本中心估計 2026/2027 年南電營收 599.8/1095.4 億元，
年增 49.3%/82.6%，毛利率 26.9%/43.4%，營業利益 141.3/442.3 億元，年增 612.7%/213.1%，
稅後獲利 117/366.9 億元，年增 501.2%/213.5%，EPS 18.11/56.78 元。 
綜合上述，南電高階產品比重提升、稼動率維持高檔及報價上調帶動下，預期 ABF／BT 毛利率可
望持續改善，整體營運動能於 2027 年明顯增強。評價方面，南電歷史本益比交易區間約介於 6–36
倍；考量本輪 ABF／BT 供需結構改善、高階產品組合升級，以及稼動率快速提升所帶來的毛利率
擴張，本中心給予 2027 年 30 倍本益比評價，以 2027 年預估 EPS 推算目標價 1,700 元，並給予
買進投資建議。

公司簡介  
南亞電路板成立於 1997 年，前身為南亞塑膠自 1985 年開始營運的電路板事業部，1998 年正式獨立營運，並於 2006 年在
台灣證券交易所掛牌上市，為台塑集團旗下主要 PCB 與 IC 載板廠商，目前南亞塑膠持股約 67%。南亞電路板     年報
_8046 南電_2025 年報(1) 公司產品涵蓋傳統多層印刷電路板、HDI、軟硬複合板（Rigid-Flex），以及 ABF 與 PP／BT IC
載板，生產據點主要位於台灣錦興、樹林及中國昆山。南亞電路板 其中 IC 載板為公司主要高階產品布局，應用涵蓋伺服器
CPU、AI ASIC、高效能運算（HPC）、800G／1.6T 高速交換器、記憶體、SSD 控制晶片、SiP、行動裝置及車用電子等
領域；一般 PCB 則持續往 AI 伺服器、GPU 加速卡、伺服器與高階網通等高附加價值產品升級。    年報_8046 南電_2025
年報(1) 公司近年營運重心進一步向 AI 與 HPC 相關高階載板移動，受惠 AI 晶片朝大尺寸、高層數及高 I/O 規格發展，以及
CSP 持續擴大自研 ASIC 布局，南電持續強化高階 ABF 載板、1.6T 交換器及 AI 伺服器相關產品布局，並規劃新增高階載
板產能，以支應下一階段 AI 基礎建設需求。

### Page 16

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：PCB 2026 年 10 月 5 日 Nan Ya PCB 第16 頁，共 19頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 南電整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低等風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於略為領先
的位置，在電子零組件行業的公司中排名略微領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 南電的整體曝險屬於低等水準，略為領先電子零組件行業的平均水準。  
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：南電在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司訊息揭露能力非常優良，
提供投資人及市場充足的資訊。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

Document 131: AI双视角：行业捕获率与价值创造率

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI双视角：行业捕获率与价值创造率.pdf
Report source: 国金证券
Date: 2026-10-09
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

敬请参阅最后一页特别声明 1

基本内容： 
在此前的《AI洪流三部曲》中，我们尝试给出了AI技术所影响的劳动力规模以及对应的薪资水平；但在理解潜在收
入上限时依然缺少关键一环，即 AI 技术的增效水平，进而量化潜在可代替的支出水平。除此之外，行业视角下捕获
率的高低也越发关键，尤其是当AI收入焦点从单一的Coding转向科研（AI4S） 、办公场景、文娱传媒等更广泛领域。

AI技术捕获率与价值创造率实际上是站在不同视角看待技术发展： 对于模型商而言， 捕获率决定着未来的潜在收入水
平；对于使用AI的企业而言，价值创造率决定着AI在纯替代视角下能享受的降本增效水平。前者对应着短期的市场
景气度和硬件侧的交易空间，而后者则对应着更长期的数据中心回报率和模型收入能力。

不同行业的捕获率差异巨大，计算机行业不到10%，金融行业不到2%；AI平均增效约7 倍，计算机行业享受约四分之
一的“价值创造”。

在数据允许的情况下，未来有三个非常重要的观察视角。

首先是价值创造水平，因为当前AI产业自证需要的是可量化的收入，但是大量的福利通过消费者剩余的方式被AI使
用者所享受，如何定量这些水平，而非简单盯住ARR和硬件订单，会成为从硬件交易向软件/应用过渡的重要参考。

其次是薪资水平的参考尺度，尽管在短期尺度 AI 在大多数行业依然是替代型技术，但是现代社会的多任务属性决定
着劳动力薪资或许并不是当前AI收入的主要来源，换言之对于AI的劳动力冲击可以相对乐观一些。

最后是研究方法的缺乏，如果AI 真的用以创造，那 BLS 视角下的 755 个职业，以及背后对应的数千个任务属性是否
能够准确描述 AI 在当前社会正在做什么？如果越来越多的任务被划归为“无法定义” ，如果AI 成为一个多模态，多
任务并行处理的工具，我们讨论的就不仅仅是某个职业或者某个行业。

金融、计算机、文娱传媒与科学研究完全可以在某个时间被 AI 所融合，这或许才是未来软件应用所追求的方向：上
限不是单一行业的单一薪资，而是全社会的总价值创造能力。

风险提示 
1）宏观经济数据频率较低，对于跨境行业数据估计存在滞后性；2）美国产业政策受党派影响明显，执政党派的更换
可能引发产业政策回溯；3）AI技术发展与产业投资存在较大不确定性。

### Page 3

宏观经济点评

敬请参阅最后一页特别声明 3

扫码获取更多服务 
在此前的《AI洪流三部曲》中，我们尝试给出了AI技术所影响的劳动力规模以及对应的薪资水平；但在理解潜在收
入上限时依然缺少关键一环，即 AI 技术的增效水平，进而量化潜在可代替的支出水平。除此之外，行业视角下捕获
率的高低也越发关键，尤其是当AI收入焦点从单一的Coding转向科研（AI4S）、办公场景、文娱传媒等更广泛领域。 
AI技术捕获率与价值创造率实际上是站在不同视角看待技术发展： 对于模型商而言， 捕获率决定着未来的潜在收入水
平；对于使用AI的企业而言，价值创造率决定着AI在纯替代视角下能享受的降本增效水平。前者对应着短期的市场
景气度和硬件侧的交易空间，而后者则对应着更长期的数据中心回报率和模型收入能力。 
尤其是最近的 Muse 应用可能采用“抽成”的商业模式（类似瓦特改良蒸汽机的收入来自于煤炭节约量的三分之一），
可以理解为在当前这个Agent模态下进行技术改良，这也更加说明理解“AI效率提升”的重要性。 
我们根据 Anthropic 的公开数据，重新构造了美国全量职业视角下的AI 维度，补充了职业/行业视角下 AI 的增效倍
率、AI技术所创造的价值分布以及不同行业的捕获率水平——这三个关键计算结果。由于数据有限，以及统计口径的
原因，在部分指标估算中存在较大偏离，在第三部分我们将进行充分讨论。 
一、不同行业的捕获率差异巨大，计算机行业不到10%，金融行业不到 2% 
计算即时捕获率，以及不同行业潜在空间的计算方法并不复杂：只需要用ARR收入乘不同行业使用率作为行业实际收
入贡献，将结果与实际暴露度下对应的薪资总数对比，即可得到行业视角下捕获率的近似描述。 
然而，该方法的难点在于Token消耗量的行业分布数据（以及对应价格）难以挖掘；我们只能找到Anthropic公布的
“以任务次数调用”统计的使用率数据。这意味着一次计算机行业任务与餐饮服务业任务调用含有相等权重，但现实
情况是计算机行业单次任务消耗的 Token或是其他行业的数十倍。由于这种有偏对应(只能用“调用次数”而不是“消
耗token数”来代表AI收入)，导致部分行业（例如金融业）的捕获率可能偏低。 
首先，我们“再次”展示截止到2026年3 月（最后的数据更新月份），行业视角下的实际技术暴露度与理论技术暴露
度水平。理论上最有可能被AI替代的行业是计算机与数学(87.6%）、商业与金融（78.2%）以及法律(78.0%），但实际
观察到的暴露度排序与理论值并不一致， 实际暴露度最高的行业是计算机与数学(35.3%）、办公室与行政支持 （33.2%）
以及销售相关职位(24.6%）。 
对于这个暴露度数值，我们再次强调这并不能理解为有35%的员工已经失业，或者有 35%的任务已经可以 AI 自动化，
而应该理解为“将每一个职业拆解成不同的任务后，以完成每个任务的时间加权的情况下，有 35%的任务已经在
Anthropic的AI使用中被观察到” 。

接着，我们展示以任务次数为权重的统计方式下，美国不同行业的AI使用率占比（包括API调用和订阅端） ：计算机
行业占据了约25%的使用次数（尽管仍被严重低估） ，文体传媒业接近15%，而金融行业仅占5%——单次任务越复杂，

扫码获取更多服务 
环节越多，调用的Token也会越多，而金融业采用AI的任务更多以“大篇幅”为主，占比自然较低。

基于以上数据，我们对于捕获率水平进行直接的计算：因为数据本身只更新至5 月，我们假设 AI 大模型的总 ARR 收
入在 800 亿美元水平，得到了捕获率的结果如下：计算机行业的捕获率大约在9%，而金融行业的捕获率仅在 1.9%。
我们可以从数学计算的角度找到一个“解释” （但并非完全合理） ：金融行业的分子过小，即使用率水平低；但分母过
大，24.5%的实际暴露度水平对应着约2500亿美元的总薪资水平。

扫码获取更多服务

最后，我们也可以对未来潜在捕获收入进行一个估计，即用当前不同行业的捕捉水平去对标未来理论暴露薪资收入。
在这个纯替代视角下，潜在捕获收入在4000 亿美元水平，其中计算机行业贡献500 亿美元，建筑工程和文体传媒位
居第二和第三位，分别为410 和 360 亿美元。虽然金融行业贡献的 AI 收入被低估，但就整体数值和行业排序来看，
基本也符合产业发展逻辑。 
一方面，4000亿美元基本是替代视角下ARR收入的某种边界共识。另一方面，除了计算机外，建筑工程（辅助设计）
与运输搬运（自动驾驶、运力规划）的收入贡献排名靠前，与 AI 的流程优化有关；而文体传媒与教培行业则体现出
了AI的“定制能力” 。 
图4：总潜在捕获收入约 4000亿美元

来源：Anthropic Economic Index，国金证券研究所 
二、AI 平均增效约 7 倍，计算机行业享受约四分之一的“价值创造” 
接下来，我们换到AI使用者的视角，讨论对于那些使用AI的企业/职业/任务，技术本身带来了多大的效率提升。如
果Meta公布的Muse真的要采用抽成而非订阅模式，这会成为未来理解Agent发展的新关键。这里我们从“消费者剩
余”的角度来理解接下来要展示的数据——当使用者付给模型商的水平恒定之后，AI创造的额外价值即为 AI技术带
给消费者的“消费者剩余” 。 
根据 Anthropic公布的全量数据， 我们得到了美国755个职业 （部分空缺） 对应的每次使用AI完成任务的平均时间，
同时 Anthropic也公布了反事实估计值，即如果这个任务由人类自主完成，所需要的平均时间。基于这两个数据，我
们可以得到初步的AI增效倍率，再根据每一个职业的使用占比和对应的时薪水平，我们可以得到应用AI技术后，行
业视角下的价值创造分布情况。 
我们通过举例方式进行简要说明：电脑程序员(行业名称：Computer Programmer，职业编号：15-1251)，使用AI情
况下平均任务时间为52.35分钟，不使用AI 完成时间（估计值）为9.12小时，即547.2分钟——AI的增效倍率则为
547.2/52.35 = 10.45。在对每一个职业进行类似统计后， 以使用占比作为权重加权可以得到行业视角下的增效倍率。 
自下而上的计算整合后，我们观察到使用AI技术平均增效7 倍，增效最明显的是行政支持行业，约10.3倍，最低的
是社会服务和医疗，仅2.8倍。计算机行业大约在7.2倍，而金融行业在22个行业增效倍率排名第二，高达8.7 倍。

从职业视角来看（排除使用率为 0 的职业），AI带来的效率提升最明显的职业是微生物学家(20.96 倍)，前20当中包
括了前沿科学领域，行政支持领域以及艺术行业。这也比较符合当前对AI发展方向的期待，科研是AI技术一个新的
扩展领域，尤其是对于大量可重复，有方法论的研究来说，AI的降本增效更加明显——近期数学界千禧年问题的持续
突破，以及数百个前沿问题的集中解决是一个很好的例证。

三、AI 的价值创造与“消费者剩余” 
有了每个职业的增效水平，以及 AI 使用率占比后，我们可以计算职业/行业视角下的 AI 价值创造分布。依然以计算
机程序员为例进行说明，已知其使用 AI 情况下平均任务时间为 52.35 分钟，不使用 AI 完成时间（估计值）为 9.12
小时，即547.2分钟，因此使用AI节约了494.85分钟（8.25小时） ，则可以粗略的理解为AI单次任务的价值创造为
8.25小时乘以时薪。 
问题在于，我们只知道使用率分布，不知道具体调用次数，因此无法知道每一个职业/行业具体的价值创造水平，但

扫码获取更多服务 
可以算出价值创造的分布——即每个行业分了“多少比例”的 AI价值创造蛋糕，但是不知道这个蛋糕“有多大”。 
单一职业的价值创造比例为 Share𝑖，其中N 作为总次数会被约分，使用率、时间以及每个职业对应的时薪此前已举例
说明： 
𝑉𝑖 = 𝑁 × UsageRate𝑖 × (HumanTime𝑖 − AITime𝑖) × HourlyWage𝑖 
Share𝑖 = 𝑉𝑖∑ 𝑉𝑗𝐼𝑗=1  
经过计算，计算机行业获得了 AI 价值创造（消费者剩余）的 25%，文体传媒行业也获得了 AI 价值创造的近 15%，管
理、教育培训也都是使用AI的主要受益者。AI价值创造的行业排序也符合AI交易的热点方向：比如Coding的场景
应用之后， 近期火热的AI短剧行业体现了AI在文体传媒行业中的价值创造， 培训行业 （包括教培、人力资源培训等）
也可以通过AI进行更加个性化/定制化的服务。 
图7：计算机行业享受约四分之一的“价值创造”

来源：Anthropic Economic Index，BLS，国金证券研究所 
有了以上数据后，我们可以把行业的使用率与价值创造率进行对比，进而勾勒出AI使用的“性价比水平”，即是否价
值创造率高的行业使用较多。 
总的来说，两者基本匹配，这意味着AI价值创造倍率高的行业也基本对应更多的AI技术使用。但其中出现了两个相
对例外，一个是管理行业，用约 5.9%的任务次数创造了约 13.6%的“消费者剩余”，这主要依赖该行业的高附加值属
性；另一个是销售行业，用了约9.3%的任务次数，只创造了4.4%的额外价值。 
当然， 我们强调这些估计都是以“消费者剩余”的视角， 最终这些价值以什么样方式转化（是增加劳动者的休闲时间，
还是增加资本家的投资回报） ，依然存在较大不确定性。

扫码获取更多服务 
图8：从价值创造/使用率的角度，各行业基本匹配

来源：Anthropic Economic Index，BLS，国金证券研究所 
我们再次强调的是，因为缺少总调用次数，无法直接计算这些消费者剩余的绝对规模，这是个较为遗憾的地方。 
这里我们引用一些前沿论文的估算：Brynjolfsson等人的2026年4 月论文测算(What is Generative AI Worth)：
站在 2025 年末的节点，全球生成式 AI 的收入约 142 亿美元，对应的美国消费者剩余约 1162 亿美元，即倍率约为
1162/142 = 8.2倍（假设绝大多数都发生在美国） ；这与Anthropic估算的约7 倍的AI技术平均增效水平较为接近。 
当然两者的视角并不一致，Brynjolfsson等人的论文主要是基于AI收入，而我们则通过节约时间与薪资倒推；但大
致的消费者剩余规模可能就在5-10倍的量级水平。 
总的来说，目前AI创造的大部分消费福利仍由用户而非厂商获得，AI已经创造了很大的隐性价值，但产业收入和利
润对这些价值的捕获仍明显滞后——但是如果 AI 不可避免地从硬件交易转向关注软件，这个“价格创造倍率（消费
者剩余） ”视角将更加重要。 
四、以上估算所存在的弊端，以及未来观察视角 
我们最后单独讨论捕获率视角与价值创造视角估算所存在的弊端， 这并不只因为某些估计中呈现出了极大的方差水平，
更是希望引发对未来观察AI收入水平尺度的一些思考。 
正如前文所反复强调的那样，我们的核心估计主要依赖于任务次数的分布，而非Token消耗量，天然低估了一些高收
入创造能力行业（比如计算机、金融等）的占比，进而低估了AI技术的潜在捕获率。 
还有对捕获率的恒定假设（外推到理论总薪资），也限制了替代型AI技术的潜在收入空间——因为随着模型能力的快
速增长，以及技术自然渗透的S曲线，捕获率会随着时间推移而逐渐上升。 
再深一层的思考，薪资是衡量替代型AI技术潜在收入的度量尺度，但创造型AI技术已经出现，同时过去普遍担忧的
广泛裁员似乎并未发生，那么是否有衡量AI未来收入的更好尺度？这个问题需要思考。 
其次是技术暴露度的度量意义或被高估，Agent进入到第四个季度，尽管美国信息技术业的裁员依然在缓慢推进，但
总的来说我们并没有看到预想中的劳动力冲击。根本问题是观察到任务被暴露，并不等于 AI 能自主完全执行任务，
因此暴露度高估了工资池范围（实际薪资暴露），进而低估了捕获率水平（薪资作为分母被高估） 。 
另一个高估技术暴露度的因素来自于当前更多职业都具备多任务属性(multi-task)； 当前社会的主要工作岗位都对应
着多个任务，因此在职业主任务中AI 带来的效率提升/技术暴露并不能完全等效于整个职业对AI 技术的反馈。且大
多数岗位都存在多任务串联的特性，即任务链条存在前置条件；这些都意味着从技术到效率再到就业与收入的影响并
非按比例传导，而是有所损耗。

扫码获取更多服务 
图9：AI 场景中所观察到的多任务情景比例

来源：Anthropic Economic Index，BLS，国金证券研究所 
我们理解，在数据允许的情况下，未来有三个非常重要的观察视角。 
首先是价值创造水平，因为当前AI产业自证需要的是可量化的收入，但是大量的福利通过消费者剩余的方式被AI使
用者所享受，如何定量这些水平，而非简单盯住ARR和硬件订单，会成为从硬件交易向软件/应用过渡的重要参考。 
其次是薪资水平的参考尺度，尽管在短期尺度 AI 在大多数行业依然是替代型技术，但是现代社会的多任务属性决定
着劳动力薪资或许并不是当前AI收入的主要来源，换言之对于AI的劳动力冲击可以相对乐观一些。 
最后是研究方法的缺乏，如果AI 真的用以创造，那 BLS 视角下的 755 个职业，以及背后对应的数千个任务属性是否
能够准确描述 AI 在当前社会正在做什么？如果越来越多的任务被划归为“无法定义” ，如果AI 成为一个多模态，多
任务并行处理的工具，我们讨论的就不仅仅是某个职业或者某个行业。金融、计算机、文娱传媒与科学研究完全可以
在某个时间被 AI 所融合，这或许才是未来软件应用所追求的方向：上限不是单一行业的单一薪资，而是全社会的总
价值创造能力。 
风险提示 
1）AI技术数据更新频率较低，无法及时反馈模型能力的大幅提升；2）美国产业政策受党派影响明显，执政党派的更
换可能引发产业政策回溯；3）AI技术发展与产业投资存在较大不确定性。

Wired for growth
Initiate coverage with an Outperform rating and target price of NT$270
ACES is entering a two-engine growth phase. It is a key power cable supplier to
Bloom Energy as Bloom doubles its fuel-cell capacity, and it is expanding its high-
speed connector content from AI server daughterboards to ASIC motherboards, as

a high-speed cable leg comes on stream. We forecast a 72% EPS Cagr over 2026-
28, with OPM expanding from 7.4% in 2026 to 11.0% in 2027. Our TP of NT$270
is based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, and we initiate coverage at Outperform.
Bloom Energy the key earnings driver in 2027
ACES's multiyear partnership with Bloom locks in its share as Bloom plans to lift
capacity from 2GW in 2026 to 4GW/6GW in 2027/28, based on our checks. ACES
has shipped fuel-cell power cables to Bloom since 1Q26 and will add external DLO

high-voltage cables, with ASP of US$80-100k per set. We expect Bloom power
cables to be NT$5.8bn of sales and account for 28% of group revenue in 2027.
Power products need little incremental R&D and benefit from strong operating
leverage. We expect overall OPM to expand from 7.4% in 2026 to 11.0% in 2027.
High-speed products a new driver
Previously, ACES supplied only daughterboard connectors, at 10-20 units per rack.
On ASIC platforms, where ODMs have more say in supplier selection, ACES has

secured the opportunity to ship both motherboard and daughterboard connectors
(about 30 units per rack) and broadens its connector product offerings. With Gen6
connectors carrying around a 15% ASP premium over Gen5, and ACES as one of
only four vendors qualified by CSPs at Gen6, we forecast server revenue growth of
107%/74% YoY in 2026/27. The newly added Gen6 PCIe cable product, qualified
by two CSPs, began shipping in April 2026 at 20% of GM, with room to rise towards

30% as yield improves. Hence, we expect GM to recover to 26% in 2027.
Capacity in place ahead of demand
ACES has committed firm capacity ahead of demand. For power cables, it has leased
a new facility next to its Philippine plant, with new capacity planned to come online
in 4Q26. For server products, it plans to raise connector capacity from 4m to 5m
units/mo and will expand high-speed cable production lines from two to eight.
Initiate coverage with an Outperform rating

We forecast 2027/28SP EPS of NT$11.19/NT$14.93, with our 2027 forecast 48%
ahead of consensus. Our TP of NT$270 is based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, backed
by a 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP driven by power cable ramp-up and high-speed
connector shipment growth. We initiate coverage with an Outperform rating.

Bloom Energy the key earnings driver in 2027
We see tremendous advantages for ACES from Bloom Energy’s strong demand backed
by its aggressive expansion plan. ACES supplies inner power cables used inside each
fuel cell in 2026 and will begin supplying external diesel locomotive (DLO) cables that
connect fuel cell stacks into a fuel cell module (energy server). Each set (including inner
cable and external cable) carries dollar content of US$80-100k.

Furthermore, ACES has worked with Bloom on high-voltage power cables for SOFC
for around six years, even though the real contribution did not transpire until 2026. On
the back of this long-standing relationship, ACES is the key supplier during the
development phase and holds at least a 30% share in the mass production stage.
Together with these considerations, we expect inner cables to contribute revenue of
NT$5,113m/NT$8,074m in 2027/28, while external cables will add revenue of

NT$707m/NT$1,154m.
Figure 1
Power cable revenue from Bloom Energy and YoY change
Source: SinoPac
As power scarcity remains the key bottleneck for AI datacentre construction, Bloom
serves as a suitable solution and offers fuel cell systems that generate electricity from
natural gas. Its modular, on-site, 24/7 power platform stands apart in the power sector.
Against a conventional grid built on centralised generation, Bloom delivers compelling
time-to-power, cleaner air and water emissions profile, native-DC compatibility with

emerging 800V AI datacentre architectures, and a solution that communities and
nearby residents accept more readily.

infrastructure, with Bloom providing its fuel cell-based power generation systems and
Brookfield providing capital and infrastructure development capabilities. The strategic
financing framework expanded on June 30, 2026, from the original $5bn (October
2025) to $25bn, a fivefold increase. On customer wins, Nebius dropped its previously
planned gas and combustion turbines in May 2026 and switched to Bloom’s SOFC

solution, signing a 10-year master agreement worth up to $2.6bn. The first phase
covers 328MW of installed capacity, or roughly 250MW of guaranteed capacity, and
is expected to come online within 2026. Bloom also expanded its Oracle contract from
an initial 1.2GW to up to 2.8GW and signed deals including AEP (up to 1 GW) and
Equinix (over 100 MW across 19 datacentres), broadening its customer base materially.
Wide range of power cable products
ACES supplies Bloom with a variety of power cables across different specifications,

including Power Conversion System (PCS) cables, hot box cables and Diesel
Locomotive (DLO) high-voltage cables, rather than a single product type. In 2026,
ACES only supplies PCS cables and hot box cables, while the company will expand its
product offerings to DLO high-voltage cables from 2027, which we expect to
contribute another NT$707m of sales for the whole year.
The PCS cables and hot box cables are the inner power cables. Hot box cables
interconnect individual fuel cells within the hot box, where power generation creates

significant heat and therefore requires materials with higher durability and heat
resistance. Kaori and MTAR supply hot boxes for fuel cell power generation systems,
which require ACES’s hot box cables as one of their key components.
On the other hand, PCS cables are installed inside the Power Conversion System (PCS)
and are supplied to AcBel, a PCS solution provider. The PCS converts DC output into
usable power and manages load changes. As SOFCs generate DC power with voltage

varying according to load, the output cannot be directly connected to a datacentre.
The external power cable portfolio is mainly DLO high-voltage cables, which transmit
high-voltage DC power between fuel cell platforms, power modules and microgrid
systems. As external cables need to withstand higher voltage levels, their dollar content
is expected to be slightly higher than that of inner power cables.
Lastly, ACES also ships cables directly to Bloom Energy for development purposes.

DLO high-voltage cable opportunity heading into 800V DC era
We also see DLO cables, which are currently used outside the fuel cell, as having
significant potential after introducing 800V architecture. Under conventional AC
architecture, grid power, onsite turbines and reciprocating engines supply AC

power, and the downstream transformer, switchgear and UPS cables belong to the
utility/EPC supply chain, which ACES does not address. Bloom can deliver native
DC power directly to the rack, so the DC output cables might become part of
Bloom’s system. We see this opening as a new content opportunity for ACES as a
qualified high-voltage power cable supplier.
Within Bloom’s energy server under 800V architecture, the longer series stack of
fuel cells and the replacement of the inverter with DC-DC conversion require a

redesign of the internal harness to high-voltage specifications, which we believe
positions ACES to ship a growing volume of high-voltage DLO cables over time.

High-speed products a new driver
In 3Q24, ACES passed product qualification with ODMs for the high-speed
connectors used on Nvidia GB200 networking cards (NICs) and accelerator cards,
marking its move from traditional PC peripherals into the server space and Nvidia
supply chain. In 2025, server revenue accounted for 10% of ACES’s total revenue.
Looking ahead, we expect ACES’s server business to grow 107% in 2026, thanks to

its new product offerings, higher content per rack of high-speed products, and
strong contribution from two major ASIC customers. Looking ahead, with the new
connector capacity coming online and increasing ASP driven by connector spec
upgrade, we expect server revenue to further grow 74%/33% in 2027/28SP.

Broadening connector footprint in ASICs
Since Nvidia has strict control over its supply chain, ACES previously supplied only
the daughterboard high-speed connectors for GB200/300 servers, at roughly 10 to
20 units per rack. On ASIC platforms, since ODMs have greater influence over
supplier selection, ACES secures share on both the motherboard and the
daughterboard, lifting content to around 30 units per rack.

Besides, ACES historically supplied only MCIO connectors to these ASIC customers,
but as further qualifications clear, the company will broaden its connector product
offerings in 2026 to include Multi-Track, board-to-board, and E1/E3 connectors.
We expect the contribution from ASIC customers to scale further in 2H26, driving
server revenue to grow 64% HoH.
Figure 13 Figure 14
ACES high-speed connector content per rack increase ACES provides MCIO and a wide range of high-speed connectors

Source: SinoPac Source: ACES
Riding the Gen6 ASP curve with a compatibility advantage
Aside from the spectacular growth in the connector shipments, we expect ACES to
benefit from the ongoing Gen6 connector upgrade cycle, with ASPs expected to
increase by 15%. The AI server trays currently adopt a mix of Gen5 and Gen6
connectors, but we expect the penetration of Gen6 connectors to gradually
increase going forward as the migration from Gen5 to Gen6 is the first major

generational shift since Genoa introduced Gen5 in 2022.

Figure 15
Gen6 connector ASP is 15% higher than Gen5
Source: SinoPac
Only three to four connector vendors are qualified by CSPs at Gen6, and ACES is
one of them. Furthermore, ACES can supply both Gen5 and Gen6 platforms under
its existing qualification, with no need to re-sample or re-qualify for Gen5. This is
because Gen6 connectors keep footprint backward compatibility with Gen5. By
contrast, other peers must re-sample and run through the full qualification process

again to move up to Gen6.
About the PCIe migration and Gen6 spec upgrade
PCIe has long been used for on-board and in-chassis interconnects. In the Vera
Rubin platform, PCIe Gen6 serves as the in-tray bridge to front-of-tray modules
such as CX-9 SuperNICs, BlueField-4 DPUs and NVMe storage. GPU-to-GPU scale-
up runs on NVLink, with UALink as the open alternative; these fabrics work
alongside PCIe.
In order to address ever-larger training models, the PCIe must scale with the fast-

growing size of model parameters and datasets. The move from Gen5 to Gen6 is
not a simple speed doubling, even though per-lane bandwidth rises from 32 GT/s
to 64 GT/s. It reworks the link from the coding scheme up. Gen1 through Gen5
doubled I/O speed using NRZ signaling. Gen6 switches to PAM4 four-level
signaling and, for the first time, adds FEC (Forward Error Correction), which lets the
receiver detect and correct transmission errors on its own.

Each generation doubles bandwidth while tightening signal-integrity specs, which
demands higher-precision connector manufacturing. This creates a real barrier to
entry for high-speed connector suppliers.

High-speed cables emerging as a potential driver
ACES previously supplied connectors only. After several years of effort, its Gen6 PCIe
cable has now been qualified by two CSP clients, and the company began shipping the
in-rack cable product in April 2026. We expect high-speed cable shipments to enlarge
after the new capacity comes online and more CSP orders in 2H26. Hence, we expect
high-speed cable revenue to grow from NT$268m in 2026 to NT$793/NT$982m in

2027/28.
Currently, its high-speed cable product carries gross margin of merely 20%, but it is
likely to edge up to around 30% as the scale enlarges, and yield improves.

Capacity in place ahead of demand
ACES is rapidly expanding its high-speed cable and connector capacity, with high-
speed cable production lines expected to increase from two in 2025 to eight in
2H26, while it will proactively ramp up connector monthly capacity from 4m units
in July 2026 to 5m units by the end of 2026 to address customer demand ahead of
order placement.
Supported by strong customer demand and sufficient capacity, ACES expects total

connector shipments to reach 25m units in 2H26 and 50m units in 2027,
representing a significant increase from 15m units in 1H26.
As for power cables for Bloom Energy, ACES has leased a new facility next to its
Philippine plant, with the new power cable capacity planned to come online in
4Q26.

Diversified growth engines
ACES’s networking product portfolio includes set-top boxes, WiFi devices, routers,
and other networking equipment. We expect the business to benefit from
technology upgrades and equipment replacement driven by advances in wireless
communications, IoT, cloud computing, and cybersecurity. However, tariff and
inflationary pressures, together with component shortages among Asian customers,

are likely to limit growth. We expect networking product revenue to grow by 34%
and 14% in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Figure 25 Figure 26

Automotive: rising electronic content
ACES’s product range spans ADAS, reversing radar and camera, body control, smart
cockpit, smart antenna, and high-voltage connectors and harnesses, extending into
applications such as EV charging guns. It has entered a US EV major as well as

European and Chinese Tier-1 supply chains. Going forward, it will continue to focus
on high-voltage, high-frequency, and high-speed connection technologies. The
company expects the automotive sector to stay flat in 2027 to reflect muted
demand and limited internal resource allocation, as it focuses on AI-related business
at this stage. In the long-term, growth from the automotive sector will be driven by
ADAS upgrades, which lifts demand for sensors and compute, alongside rising EV

penetration, which adds high-value electronic systems and power semiconductor
content.

Industrial: a steady UPS-driven base
ACES supplies industrial power cords, used mainly in uninterruptible power systems
(UPS). A UPS is an electrical device that acts as a reliable buffer when the main
power supply behaves abnormally, such as during a fault, outage, or unstable
voltage. It keeps automation controllers, sensors, and actuators continuously

powered so equipment keeps running. When a power problem occurs, the UPS
supplies stable backup power to prevent equipment failure, data corruption, or
system crashes.  We expect ACES’s industrial business to grow steadily, with growth
of 9% and 14% in 2026 and 2027, respectively.
Figure 29 Figure 30
ACES Industrial product portfolio Industrial sales and YoY
Source: ACES Source: SinoPac

Under the twin pressures of softening end-consumer demand and continued

supply-chain cost increases, we expect ACES’s full-year NB revenue to decrease
12% in 2026 and return to a growth trajectory in 2027. However, we remain
cautiously optimistic about 2027 with the launch of Windows 12, combined with
easing component shortages and broader AI adoption, which should drive consumer
replacement demand, lifting global NB shipments -5%/5% year over year in
2027/28, which is a gradual recovery following a weaker 2026.

Diversifying into new growth opportunities
We found that ACES is diversifying its products’ end-application by developing new
specification and engaging with new clients. Hence, we believe in its long-term

Power rack: leveraging its long-standing relationship with Schneider

ACES has a long-standing relationship with Schneider Electric at its Philippine plant.
The partnership dates back more than 20 years, with ACES’s Philippine plant
initially serving Schneider almost exclusively. Over the years, the product portfolio
has expanded from conventional power cables to uninterruptible power systems
(UPS) and, more recently, power racks for AI datacentres.
ACES began shipping high-voltage power cables for power racks in 1Q26 and

entered the volume ramp-up phase in 2Q26. We remain positive on power rack
adoption as the PSU in every IT tray hits a physical limit as rack power reaches
1MW, occupies rack space and caps compute density. Moving power shelves and
BBU/CBU into a dedicated power rack decouples power from computing and frees

Initiate with an Outperform rating
We initiate coverage of ACES with an Outperform rating given its dual growth
engine from Bloom’s strong expansion and high-speed connector customer wins
and generation upgrades. We forecast 2027/28 EPS of NT$11.19/NT$14.93, with
our 2027 forecast 48% ahead of consensus. Our TP of NT$270 is based on 20x
2H27-1H28SP PE, backed by a 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP driven by Bloom’s

significant ramp and larger content in server products. Initiate coverage with an
Outperform rating.
Company profile
ACES was founded in 1996, and it mainly produces connectors, micro-coaxial cable,
high-speed transmission cables, and RF connectors. Connectors and cable
assemblies link two separate subsystems or operating units and carry the signals
between them, allowing the overall system to function properly. They are used
across a wide range of electronic products, with main applications spanning

notebooks and PC peripherals, consumer electronics, automotive electronics, cloud
servers, networking equipment, and industrial equipment.
To keep pace with the push towards lighter, thinner, and smaller notebooks and
consumer devices, ACES developed high-temperature-resistant, fine-pitch, and
low-profile products at an early stage. At the same time, it is moving actively into
the high-frequency, high-speed connectors, high-speed cables, and power supplies

required for cloud servers, as well as the connector and cable assemblies used in
automotive electronics.

Company development
ACES has used M&A well to gain new capabilities, plants and technology, which in
turn opened doors to new customers and markets. In 2015, it bought MEC IMEX
to add cable assembly capability and a plant in the Philippines, which took it into
the industrial market. In 2017, the CPC deal gave it access to the European auto
connector market and a plant in Vietnam. In 2019, it bought Kuang Ying to add

metal stamping capacity and reach Taiwan telecom customers. The key deal came
in 2021, when ACES bought 100% of Genesis, a US high-speed interconnect maker
based in Atlanta. The main goal was to gain Genesis’s long-standing ties with US
CSP and cloud customers. In 2025, ACES opened its Phase Two R&D headquarters
in Taoyuan, which focuses on cloud connectors and high-speed cables. We believe
10 years of M&A have turned ACES from a consumer connector maker into a

supplier of high-speed interconnect solutions for AI servers and the cloud.

ACES’s shareholding structure is relatively stable, with the top 10 owning about

Cashflow and balance sheet
ACES’s balance sheet shows healthy debt exposure with an improving net cash
level. Given its plan to massively expand capacity at its Taiwan plants, we expect
Capex to remain at a high level of NT$1,075m in 2026 and then slightly decline to
NT$855m in 2027. We forecast its depreciation-to-sales ratio to be around 3% in

2027, which is at the low end of its historical level.
We expect ACES’s ROE to expand from 10% in 2026 to 21% in 2027 and 23% in
2028, as the volume ramp of AI high-speed connectors and Bloom Energy power
cables drives a richer product mix and gross margin expansion, delivering 121% EPS
growth in 2027.
We expect ACES’s FCF to turn positive in 2026 as operating cashflow scales with
the AI high-speed connector and Bloom power cable ramp while Capex normalises

after the 2026 capacity buildout. Looking ahead, we expect ACES’s free cashflow
per share to be NT$0.8-1.5 in coming years.

Competitive positioning

In high-speed connectors, ACES competes primarily with overseas peers including
Amphenol (APH), TE, and Molex. APH’s edge lies in a development-technology lead of
one to two generations, while ACES’s relative strength rests on customers’ recognition
on delivering quality comparable to the overseas majors, alongside more flexible lead
times, strong capacity-expansion support, and fast ramp-up.
The structural opportunity comes from the supply chain integration trend. As

transmission speeds rise, CSPs increasingly prefer suppliers which can integrate high-
speed connectors with high-speed cables and ship assembled, finished goods.

### Page 29

Wired for growth ACES - O-PF
6 October 2026 29
ACES is one of the few Taiwanese players with this vertical-integration footprint: it
holds wire-drawing technology transferred in-house from ITRI, produces everything
from wire drawing through assembly on its own, and its commitment to moving into
high-speed cables has been noted by AWS and Meta, backed by roughly two years of
qualification work already completed.
By contrast, other Taiwanese peers have yet to build comparable vertical integration.

Customers have historically split connectors and cables across separate suppliers, with
cable suppliers represented by BizLink (3665 TT) and Longwell (6290 TT), while peers
such as Lotes (3533 TT), Alltop (3526 TT), Argosy (3217 TT) and JPC Connectivity
(6197 TT) are likewise strong in connectors but lack in-house cable capability.
Since the Gen5 qualified-supplier base was already fragmented, the Gen6 bar of
combined connector-plus-cable capability becomes ACES’s entry point, bringing ACES

into design-in from Gen6. The qualified connector supplier base is small, with suppliers
including APH, TE, and ACES, with Lotes added subsequently.
Peer comparison
ACES’s primary local competitors in connectors include Lotes, Alltop, Argosy and JPC
Connectivity. In the cable industry, it competes with Bizlink and Longwell. ACES’s
foreign peers include Amphenol, TE and FIT.

Target price calculation
We initiate coverage of ACES with an O-PF rating and a target price of NT$270,
based on 20x 2H27-1H28SP EPS, supported by a strong 72% EPS Cagr in 2026-

28SP. We use a PE-based methodology for ACES, in line with other connector
companies. We initiate coverage with an Outperform rating.
Our target multiple of 20x is in line with the 2027SP average PE of 20.4x for Taiwan-
listed connector and cable peers with direct AI datacentre exposure, namely
BizLink, Lotes and JPC Connectivity. We view this as a reasonable anchor. ACES
offers faster earnings growth than the group, with 2027SP EPS growth of 121%

versus the peer average of 52%, while its 2027-28SP ROE of 21-24% sits at the low
end of the peer range of 22-52%.

We also believe the target PE premium is justified as the AI revenue contribution,
including AI data transmission and power, rises from 4% in 2024 to 48% in 2027SP.
The company maintains its key supplier position at Bloom as the company ramps up
its capacity expansion, while ASIC customer wins and higher connector content
from the Gen6 upgrade cycle should provide additional growth drivers. As the
company gradually shifts away from its legacy consumer electronics base and its

exposure to cyclical downturns, we see room for a structural rerating.

Key risks
We see four key operational risks for ACES:
Raw material cost volatility: Copper prices drive a large share of Cogs for ACES's
cable products, while connectors carry far less exposure. ACES currently reprices
its cable products with customers on a quarterly basis, which has allowed it to

sustain a long-term GM of around 20% on power cables. However, the pass-through
might be lagging, so sharp copper price swings could cause quarterly GM to
fluctuate before repricing impact catches up.
Delays in ODM pull-in: ACES ships its high-speed connectors directly to ODM
customers. Because rack assembly sits at the final stage of the AI server supply
chain, any upstream disruption, such as components shortages and assembly yield
issue, could push out rack shipment schedules or reduce volumes. This could in turn

lead ODMs to cut or defer orders for high-speed connectors from ACES, creating
quarter-to-quarter volatility in server revenue.
Slower-than-expected ramp-up at Bloom Energy: Power cables for Bloom will
account for 28%/35% of ACES's revenue in 2027/28SP, which makes Bloom's
ramp-up the single largest swing factor in our forecasts. Delays could arise on both
the supply and demand sides. On the supply side, Bloom could fall behind its
capacity plan if new facility fit-out, equipment installation or hiring takes longer

than expected. On the demand side, its datacentre customers could defer
deployments due to grid interconnection, gas supply or financing hurdles, or due to
regulatory headwinds such as tighter permitting and emissions rules for on-site gas-
fired generation, utility restrictions on behind-the-meter power, or changes to fuel
cell tax incentives. Either scenario would directly reduce ACES's power cable
shipments. As volumes scale, Bloom may also qualify a second cable supplier, which

would add pressure to ACES's share and ASP.
Prolonged NB weakness: NB and Consumer remains ACES's largest segment at 35%
of 2026SP revenue, and our forecasts assume it returns to 4%/12% growth in
2027/28SP on a Windows 12-driven replacement cycle. If end-demand stays soft
or the replacement cycle slips, a further NB decline could offset part of the growth
from Bloom and AI servers and dilute group GM.

### Page 31

Wired for growth ACES - O-PF
6 October 2026 31
Investment thesis
ACES is evolving from a notebook connector maker into a supplier of AI
interconnects and power cables, with two growth engines ramping at the same
time. The first is Bloom Energy. ACES has co-developed SOFC power cables with
Bloom for about six years, and it is currently one of three suppliers of Bloom's power
cable assemblies, with around one-third of the total. It ships inner PCS and hot-box

cables in 2026 and adds external DLO high-voltage cables from 2027, with order
visibility extending to 2028 and beyond. The second engine is AI/ASIC servers. On
ASIC platforms, ODMs have more say in supplier selection, so ACES wins both
motherboard and daughterboard sockets, which lifts content from 10-20 to ~30
units per rack. Gen6 raises connector ASP by ~15%, and a new in-chassis PCIe Gen6
cable line, qualified by two CSPs, adds a higher-content stream. ACES integrates

vertically from wire drawing to final assembly, which makes it one of the few
Taiwanese suppliers able to ship connector-plus-cable solutions as CSPs
consolidate their vendor base.
Catalysts
(1) Bloom's capacity ramp from 2GW to 4GW/6GW in 2026-28 and new data-
center wins (Oracle, Nebius, AEP, Equinix) converting into cable orders; (2) DLO
high-voltage cable shipments starting in 2027; (3) ASIC customer ramps in 2H26
and a broader product range beyond MCIO into Multi-Track, board-to-board and

E1/E3 connectors; (4) rising Gen6 penetration on AI server trays; (5) high-speed
cable lines expanding from two to eight by end-2026, with GM improving from
~20% toward ~30%.
Valuation details
We adopt a PE-based valuation methodology as we believe the market will mainly
focus on the earnings growth outlooks of high-speed connector makers, which is
better reflected with PE metrics. We assign a target multiple of 20x PE, more than
+1sd of the three-year average, to reflect a strong outlook with margin expansion

and an EPS Cagr of 72% in 2026-28 on solid Bloom Energy demand and increasing
high speed connector content.
Investment risks
There could be downside risk to sales growth and margins if: (1) Bloom's
deployments or backlog conversion slow on financing, gas supply or permitting
delays, or ACES loses share among Bloom's cable suppliers; (2) ASIC programs ramp
more slowly or CSP Capex is cut; (3) Gen6 adoption is slower than expected, or
competition from Amphenol, TE, Molex and Lotes pressures ASP; (4) high-speed

cable yield and GM improve more slowly than we assume; (5) NB and consumer
demand weakens further on component shortages, tariffs and inflation; (6) TWD
appreciation or higher copper prices squeeze margins.

### Page 1

Doanh thu 2Q2026 tăng trưởng chậm 
nhưng biên lợi nhuận gộp vẫn được 
duy trì do tăng giá bán

Sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 chững lại 
do tâm lý nhập hàng thận trọng và 
hoạt động xây dựng phục hồi chậm

Biên lợi nhuận gộp được dự báo sẽ 
thu hẹp cuối năm, phản ánh chi phí 
nguyên liệu cao hơn trong 1Q2026

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu/LNST 2Q2026 đạt lần lượt 1,320/367 tỷ VND (+1%/+11% YoY). Lũy kế 
6M2026, doanh thu đạt 2,777 tỷ VND (+3% YoY) trong khi LNST ước đạt 671 tỷ VND 
(+9% YoY). Biên lãi gộp 6M2026 cải thiện đạt 47.0% (+240 bps YoY), chủ yếu do việc 
tăng giá bán giúp doanh nghiệp bù đắp được mức tăng của chi phí đầu vào.

Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck. Việc 
BMP thu hẹp các chương trình chiết khấu, với mức chiết khấu bình quân khoảng 
5.2% trong 2Q2026, làm giảm động lực nhập hàng của các đại lý và khiến sản lượng 
tiêu thụ chậm lại. Dù vậy, thị phần toàn quốc của BMP ước đạt 28% (+1 đpt svck) 
nhờ các doanh nghiệp khác trong ngành gặp khó khăn khi giá hạt nhựa tăng mạnh.

Biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP dự kiến thu hẹp trong 2H2026 trước áp lực chi phí 
nguyên liệu đầu vào tăng. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi kỳ vọng mức độ ảnh hưởng sẽ thấp 
hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành do (1) các lô nguyên liệu giá cao từ 
1Q2026 đã được đưa vào sản xuất và tiêu thụ một phần trong 1H2026; (2) BMP chủ 
động nâng gấp đôi hàng tồn kho để ứng phó với biến động bất lợi của giá nguyên 
liệu; (3) hơn 90% nhu cầu nguyên liệu uPVC được cung ứng từ các đối tác trong 
nước như hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam, AGC Chemicals và LSP; và (4) công ty duy 
trì giá bán bình quân ở mức cao.

Dựa trên kết quả kinh doanh và định giá, chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP với cổ 
phiếu BMP, giá mục tiêu là 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu (tương ứng với mức tăng 14.3% 
so với giá đóng cửa ngày 23/09/2026).

Tăng trưởng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất Hoạt động kinh doanh

Nhựa Bình Minh (BMP) là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực sản xuất ống nhựa với thị phần 
dẫn đầu tại thị trường nội địa (27% vào cuối 
2023), thị trường chính tập trung tại khu vực 
miền Nam. BMP có 4 nhà máy với tổng công suất 
đạt 150,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: BMP, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Kỳ vọng đà phục hồi của xây dựng dân dụng và bất động sản miền Nam sẽ thúc đẩy sản lượng tiêu 
thụ tăng trưởng trong 2026–2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Giá PVC được kỳ vọng sẽ hạ nhiệt và khả năng giữ giá bán đầu ra ở mức cao giúp BMP giữ biên lợi 
nhuận gộp ở nền cao. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

### Page 4

4

Nhu cầu nhập hàng thận trọng và 
cắt giảm các đợt chiết khấu trong 
kỳ của BMP làm giảm sản lượng tiêu 
thụ trong 2Q2026

Sự hồi phục của nhu cầu xây dựng 
dân dụng và nguồn cung các dự án 
bất động sản tại miền Nam sẽ thúc 
đẩy tăng trưởng sản lượng 2H2026 
 Sản lượng tiêu thụ trong 2Q2026 ước đạt 20,100 tấn, giảm 11% svck, nâng tổng 
sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck do các yếu tố bất 
lợi từ bên ngoài như (1) giá hạt nhựa tăng cao khiến thị trường dè chừng trong 
việc nhập hàng vì lo ngại nguồn cung bất ổn (2) BMP đã thu hẹp các chương 
trình chiết khấu so với 2025 ngay trước thời điểm xung đột bùng nổ, giúp tỷ lệ 
chi phí bán hàng/doanh thu về mức 11.3%, giảm 800 bps so với 1Q2026 ở mức 
19.3%, nhưng đồng thời cũng khiến sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP suy giảm do 
chính sách bán hàng thắt chặt hơn với các đối thủ có thị phần thấp hơn.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP dần hồi phục từ 3Q2026 khi nhu 
cầu nhập hàng của các đại lý cải thiện sau giai đoạn thận trọng trong nửa đầu 
năm. Về cuối năm, hoạt động xây dựng dự kiến tăng tốc khi điều kiện thi công 
thuận lợi hơn sau mùa mưa. Bên cạnh đó, việc triển khai các dự án nhà ở tại 
TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận được kỳ vọng hỗ trợ nhu cầu ống nhựa trong giai 
đoạn tới. Theo đó, chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP lần lượt đạt 
96,695/100,125/96,750 tấn trong 2026/2027/2028.

Biểu đồ 3. Tương quan giữa tỷ lệ chi phí bán hàng/doanh 
thu và lợi nhuận thuần/sản lượng tiêu thụ  
 Biểu đồ 4. Dự phóng nguồn cung bất động sản tại Hà Nội và Hồ 
Chí Minh

Nguồn: BMP, KBSV  Nguồn: CBRE, KBSV

   BMP duy trì vòng quay dẫn đầu trong 
ngành nhờ lợi thế nguồn nguyên liệu và thị 
phần đứng đầu tại miền Nam. Tuy nhiên, 
xu hướng suy giảm các quý gần đây về 
mức 5.3 lần, giảm 0.21 điểm QoQ do nhu 
cầu tiêu thụ toàn ngành suy giảm.

### Page 5

5

Lợi thế từ hệ sinh thái công ty mẹ 
SCG và đối tác Nhật Bản giúp BMP 
có nhiều dư địa để nâng cao hiệu 
quả hoạt động

Theo chia sẻ từ ban lãnh đạo, BMP có lợi thế vì nhập nguyên liệu từ công ty mẹ 
là hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam và đối tác Nhật Bản là AGC Chemicals Vietnam, 
giúp giảm được 5% thuế nhập khẩu và chi phí vận chuyển. Điều này giúp BMP 
hưởng lợi kép khi có thể tăng được giá bán theo thị trường nhưng nguyên vật 
liệu rẻ hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành. KBSV nhận thấy định hướng 
dài hạn của BMP là cân bằng lợi ích cổ đông và khách hàng, vì vậy việc giảm giá 
bán để giành thị phần không phải lựa chọn ưu tiên của doanh nghiệp mà là 
nâng cao tỷ suất lợi nhuận/sản phẩm thành phẩm.

KBSV dự báo biên lợi nhuận gộp thu hẹp trong 2H2026 khi chi phí nguyên liệu 
giá cao từ 1H2026 được phản ánh vào giá vốn, và hồi phục trong 2027–2028 
nhờ đặc tính giữ giá bán ít thay đổi của nhóm nhựa. Chúng tôi kỳ vọng giá PVC 
resin chịu áp lực giảm từ (1) nhu cầu xây dựng tại Trung Quốc còn yếu khi thị 
trường bất động sản chưa hồi phục rõ ràng; và (2) các nhà sản xuất giải phóng 
hàng tồn kho sau mùa mưa, làm tăng sản lượng hạt nhựa PVC resin. Theo đó, 
KBSV dự phóng biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP lên 46.4%/46.7%/46.5% trong 
2026/2027/2028.

gộp của BMP

Document 215: How AI agents could reshape retail as stores move beyond sales

Source PDF: all report pdf/How AI agents could reshape retail as stores move beyond sales.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Agentic commerce, where autonomous AI agents
independently select and purchase products on behalf of
consumers, is forcing retailers to rethink their role. As AI takes
over more of the buying process, physical stores are evolving
from a sales channel into a destination for advice, service,
experience, and omnichannel support
Katinka Jongkind
A Galaxy General AI robot retail store, Shanghai. Retail is entering a new phase
of digital disruption, where agentic commerce could fundamentally change

how consumers shop
Agentic commerce fundamentally changes
the buying process
Retail is entering a new phase of digital disruption. E-commerce
has already transformed where consumers shop; agentic
commerce is set to change how they shop. Autonomous artiﬁcial
intelligence agents can search, compare and purchase products on
behalf of consumers, shifting shopping from an active task to an
increasingly automated background process.

### Page 2

Agentic commerce goes beyond AI
assistants and comparison websites
Key diﬀerences between agentic commerce, AI assistants (such as
ChatGPT and Gemini) and comparison websites
Source: ING Research
Most relevant for routine purchases, less so
for luxury goods
Agentic commerce is not equally suited to every product category.
Its greatest potential lies in repeat and replacement purchases, as
well as functional, standardised products. In these categories, AI

agents can eﬃciently compare prices, speciﬁcations, and
availability, while using consumption patterns to determine the
optimal time to buy.
Adoption is likely to be slower for luxury, trend-sensitive and
experience-driven products. In these categories, inspiration,
emotion, and personal preference remain central to the purchasing
decision, making consumers less inclined to relinquish control. AI
agents can assist by pre-selecting products that match predeﬁned

preferences, but ﬁnal purchasing decisions are likely to remain
ﬁrmly in the hands of the consumer.

### Page 3

Consumer electronics and personal care
lead the adoption of agentic commerce
Within non-food retail, agentic commerce appears particularly well
suited to consumer electronics and personal care. Both sectors are
characterised by a high degree of product standardisation and a
large share of repeat purchases, making them ideal candidates for
AI-driven purchasing.
In consumer electronics, purchasing decisions are often based on
objective and easily comparable criteria, including price, technical

speciﬁcations, and availability. This makes products such as
televisions, laptops and other electronic devices well suited to
automation, allowing AI agents to manage much of the purchasing
journey from product selection to the ﬁnal transaction.
The potential is equally signiﬁcant in personal care, where many
purchases are routine and repetitive. Products such as diapers,
shampoo, and toothpaste can be reordered automatically based
on usage patterns and preset preferences. By reducing the eﬀort

required to order everyday products, AI agents could further
accelerate the shift towards online shopping.
Agentic commerce is best suited to
consumer electronics and personal care
products
Product categories most and least suited to agentic commerce

### Page 4

Source: ING Research
Personal taste limits AI agents' potential in
fashion and living
For now, fashion and home furnishings appear less suited to fully
autonomous agentic commerce. Purchasing decisions in these
categories are often shaped by inspiration, aesthetics, personal
taste, and broader lifestyle considerations, making consumers less
willing to hand over control to an AI agent.
While AI can support product discovery and narrow down options

based on predeﬁned preferences, the ﬁnal purchase decision will
typically remain with the consumer. In these segments, AI is
therefore more likely to serve as a decision-support tool than as a
fully autonomous purchasing agent.
Chinese platforms lead adoption
Agentic commerce creates the greatest opportunities for large
global marketplaces and technology companies. These players
combine vast product assortments with the scale, data and
infrastructure needed to deploy AI agents eﬀectively. Their

integrated ecosystems, including payment solutions, logistics
networks, and data platforms, enable them to integrate AI agents
into their business operations faster and more eﬀectively.
Chinese platforms such as Alibaba and JD.com currently lead the
way, with AI agents already deeply integrated into their respective
platform networks. These agents can not only provide product

### Page 5

recommendations but also complete purchases autonomously.
Meanwhile, US technology companies including Amazon, Google,
Meta, OpenAI and Anthropic are investing heavily in the
development and rollout of AI agents, intensifying the global race
for leadership in this emerging market.
European players are structurally lagging behind their American
and Chinese competitors. Stricter regulation and less integrated
platform networks limit their ability to bundle scale, data and

services. While Zalando is among Europe’s frontrunners in AI-
powered retail, its applications are currently focused on product
discovery, comparison, and selection. Fully autonomous
purchasing has yet to become a reality.
Technology platforms are becoming the
gatekeepers of commerce
AI agents are rarely neutral actors. Embedded within commercial
ecosystems, they are inherently linked to the interests of platforms,
marketplaces and technology companies. Although agentic

commerce promises greater convenience and time savings for
consumers, it is also likely to strengthen the dominance of large
tech ﬁrms and marketplaces. These parties are becoming
increasingly inﬂuential in shaping product choices and controlling
consumer access.
Physical stores are not disappearing, but
their role is changing
How will the role of the physical store evolve in an AI-driven retail
landscape?

### Page 6

Source: ING Research
Redeﬁning the store in the age of AI
Agentic commerce is expected to accelerate the shift of sales to
online channels, putting further pressure on the existence of
physical stores. The extent of this impact will largely depend on
consumers’ willingness to delegate purchasing decisions to AI
agents.
Physical stores will nevertheless remain relevant for many non-food
retailers. Their role is shifting from sales channel to destination for

advice, service, inspiration, and support of online sales. Stores that
continue to rely mainly on transactional sales risk losing relevance
in an increasingly AI-driven retail landscape.
Retailers that successfully integrate their physical and digital
channels, deﬁne clear roles for their stores, and deploy technology
strategically can strengthen their position within the customer
journey. In a future shaped by AI-driven commerce, their role will

increasingly centre on adding value where automation cannot.

Document 341: インド中銀、３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げ､政策スタンスも転換 ～インフレを警戒した「予防的引き締め」、追加利上げに含みも慎重姿_17668989

Source PDF: all report pdf/インド中銀、３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げ､政策スタンスも転換 ～インフレを警戒した「予防的引き締め」、追加利上げに含みも慎重姿_17668989.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 10 月 7 日

1/5 
インド中銀、３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げ､政策スタンスも転換 
～インフレを警戒した「予防的引き締め」、追加利上げに含みも慎重姿勢、ルピーや株価は？～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ インド準備銀行（ＲＢＩ）は 10 月の定例会合で、政策金利を25bp 引き上げて5.50％とし、
2023 年２月以来３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げに踏み切った。政策スタンスも「中立」から「調整
された引き締め」に変更し、追加利上げの可能性を残した。これまでＲＢＩは景気に配慮し
て、利上げを回避しつつ、ルピー安への対応を進めてきた。しかし、資金流入策に伴う銀行
システムの過剰流動性という副作用も生じ、 金融市場では予防的利上げ観測が強まっていた。 
○ インフレを巡っては、原油高の一服にもかかわらず卸売物価が高止まりしており、消費者物
価もＲＢＩの目標レンジ内ながら中央値を上回っている。加えて、モンスーンの雨量不足や
エルニーニョ現象、肥料価格の高止まりなどを背景に、先行きは食料品を中心に物価上昇が
長期化する懸念がある。このためＲＢＩは、景気が堅調なうちに物価上昇を抑える「予防的
利上げ」に踏み切ったとみられる。 
○ ＲＢＩは 2026-27 年度の経済成長率見通しを＋7.1％に上方修正する一方、インフレ率見通
しも＋5.2％に引き上げた。 先行きの政策について、 利下げの可能性を否定し、 選択肢を 「利
上げ」 か 「据え置き」 に限定したものの、 追加利上げの時期や規模については明言を避けた。 
○ このため、ＲＢＩは引き締め方向に転じたが、連続的な利上げには慎重とみられる。政策の
先行きが読みづらい状況が続くなか、ルピー相場は上値の重い展開が見込まれるほか、ＲＢ
Ｉによる資金吸収策も重なり、株価（ムンバイSENSEX）も上値を抑えられる可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/5 
【RBIは３年８ヵ月ぶりの利上げ決定に加え、政策スタンスの変更で追加利上げに含み】 
インド準備銀行（ＲＢＩ）は、10 月５
～７日 に開催し た定例の金融政策委員
会において、 政策金利であるレポ金利を
25bp 引き上げて 5.50％とすることを決
定した（図１）。ＲＢＩによる利上げ決定
は 2023 年２月以来であり、３年８ヵ月
ぶりである。さらに、今回は政策スタン
スも中立（neutral）から 「調整された引
き締め（calibrated tightening）」に変
更しており、先行きの追加利上げに含み
を持たせた格好である。 
ＲＢＩは、８月の前回会合において、全会一致で金利を据え置く様子見姿勢を維持した。 しかし、
その後に公表された議事要旨では、 政策委員の間で先行きの政策運営に関する温度差が確認された
（注１）。さらに、 ＲＢＩは景気に配慮して利上げを回避する一方、 金融市場でのルピー安が輸入イ
ンフレを招く懸念が高まった。このため、ＲＢＩは６月会合で、非居住インド人に対する優遇ドル
預金制度、企業・金融機関への対外商業借入のヘッジコスト補助導入など、ルピー相場の支援策を
発表した。 政府も、 適格外国人投資家による国債売却益へのキャピタルゲイン税撤廃を発表するな
ど、海外からの資金流入を促すことでルピー相場を下支えする方針を明らかにした。 
その後、海外からの資金流入が活発化して外貨準備高は積み上がった。一方で、国内銀行にはド
ル建て預金が急拡大し、各行はＲＢＩとのスワップを通じてコストなしでルピーに交換し たため、
銀行システムに過剰流動性が発生した。このため、ＲＢＩは過剰流動性によるインフレ増幅を警戒
して、資金吸収オペ（売りオペ）を活発化したほか、為替スワップを通じてルピー資金の吸収を図
る事態に追い込まれた （注２） 。 このように、 ＲＢＩが利上げを回避しつつ、 ルピー相場の安定を目
指したことによる「副作用」に直面してきた。このため、金融市場においては、ＲＢＩが早晩「予
防的利上げ」に舵を切るとの見方が強まっていた。

注１ ８月20 日付レポート「インド中銀､政策委員の間で将来的な利上げ可能性の認識が広がる」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/654396.html） 
注２ ９月10 日付レポート「ルピー防衛の「副作用」に直面するインド中銀」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/654982.html） 
図 1 政策金利(レポ金利)の推移

（出所）CEIC, RBI より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

### Page 3

3/5 
【インフレのさらなる上振れが懸念されるなかで「予防的利上げ」に舵】 
インドは原油需要の約９割を輸入に依
存しており、 イラン情勢による原油高は
対外収支の悪化に加え、 物価上昇を招く
ことが懸念された。 米国とイランによる
停戦合意を受けて原油高が一服し、 エネ
ルギー価格の上昇圧力が後退している
ものの、８月の卸売物価上昇率は前年同
月比＋9.92％と 10％近くで推移するな
ど高止まりしている （図２） 。このため、
川上段階においてはインフレが顕在化
するとともに、 長期化している様子がう
かがえる。 
インドでは、 米国の関税引き上げによ
る悪影響の緩和を目的に、2025 年９月に
ＧＳＴ（財・サービス税）の実質引き下
げに動いたため、 その後のインフレ率は
下振れした。 さらに、 モディ政権は国民
にパニック買いを控えて節約を呼び掛
けつつ、 景気に配慮してガソリン・軽油
価格を据え置いた。一方で、 国営石油公
社は損失を吸収して供給を維持したた
め、 金融市場ではこうした対応が財政悪
化を招くとの懸念が広がり、 ルピー相場
は調整の動きを強めて輸入インフレを招くことが警戒された。さらに、資金流出に加え、ＲＢＩが
ルピー相場の防衛を目的とする断続的な為替介入に動いたことも重なり、 外貨準備高が急減すると
の懸念も広がりをみせた。このため、モディ政権は５月、国民に在宅勤務の励行や公共交通機関の
活用による燃料需要抑制、 海外渡航や金購入の自粛による外貨準備の防衛を呼び掛けた（注３）。そ
のうえ、ガソリン・軽油価格を段階的に引き上げるなど政策転換を図った。その後のインフレ率は
加速の動きを強めており、 直近８月は前年同月比＋4.82％とＲＢＩの定める目標レンジ （４±２％）
の域内で推移しているものの、中央値を上回るなどインフレが警戒される水準にある（図３） 。 
先行きについても、 イラン情勢は不透明な状況が続いており、 エネルギー価格の高止まりを招く
可能性は高い。 そのうえ、2026 年のモンスーン （雨季） の雨量は、 インド全体では長期平均を約15％

注３ ５月12 日付レポート「インド･モディ首相､在宅勤務など燃料需要抑制策を呼び掛け」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/606977.html）
